# matplotlib 3.11 public API coverage in pltrs

Generated by `spec/api-audit/run` (enumerate.py, rust_index.py, match.py, rules*.py,
classmap.py, report.py); do not edit by hand.

matplotlib's public API was enumerated by introspection in the reference venv: every public
function, class, method, property and class attribute of the modules of the API reference
(members of private base classes such as `_AxesBase` and `_ImageBase` are listed under the
private base), the instance attributes of the central objects (Figure, Axes, Line2D, Text,
Legend, AxesImage, Colorbar, XAxis, FigureCanvasBase), and the parameters of every `Axes`,
`Axes3D` and `pyplot` method. The modules are those of the API reference, the backends'
renderers, canvases and document writers, `backend_managers`, `ft2font`, `dviread`,
`texmanager` and `testing` included. pltrs' API came from rustdoc's JSON output for
`pltrs`, `pltrs-core`, `pltrs-text`, `pltrs-render`, `pltrs-font`, `pltrs-raster` and
`pltrs-codec`.

Each item was matched automatically (same or conventional name on the pltrs type standing
for the class, the porting guide's tables, `matplotlib's ...` mentions in the docs, option
fields for parameters) and every item the matcher could not place was classified by hand;
hand rules also override automatic matches found wrong by spot checks.

* **covered**: pltrs has it; the column names the item.
* **differently**: the functionality exists in another form; the note says how.
* **n/a**: not applicable to pltrs; the note says why.
* **missing**: not in pltrs.
* Deprecated matplotlib APIs (`[*Deprecated*]` docstrings) are skipped: 56 items, listed at the end.

## Implemented by the audits

Found missing by the audit of 2026-11-02 and implemented in pltrs (Rust), with tests,
doctests and rows in the porting guide (`crates/pltrs/src/porting/`):

* `Figure::tools / tool / set_tool_keys / active_toggle`: matplotlib's tool manager: `tools`, `get_tool`, `get_tool_keymap`, `update_keymap`, `active_toggle`; `remove_tool` switches a toggle off first
* `Figure::on_tool_change / disconnect_tool_change / tool_message, ToolChange`: `toolmanager_connect`: tools added, removed and triggered, and tool messages, which the windows' status line shows
* `MovieWriter (new, grab_frame, frame_size, finish)`: matplotlib's `MovieWriter` classes: frames of a figure the program draws itself, written as an animated PNG, GIF or WebP, an HTML page or an ffmpeg video
* `Figure::to_pgf_pdf / to_pgf_png, PgfPages, PdfToPng`: the PGF backend's `print_pdf`, `print_png` and `PdfPages`: pictures typeset by `pgf.texsystem`, rasterised by pdftocairo or Ghostscript
* `testing::convert / get_file_hash / comparable_formats`: comparing PDF and PostScript files through Ghostscript, SVG files through Inkscape and GIF files, as `matplotlib.testing.compare` does
* `testing::save_diff_image / crop_to_same / make_test_filename`: the remaining helpers of `matplotlib.testing.compare`
* `testing::image_comparison (ImageComparison), testing::remove_ticks_and_titles`: `@image_comparison` and `remove_ticks_and_titles` of `matplotlib.testing.decorators`
* `TexManager::rgba (TexRgba)`: `TexManager.get_rgba`
* `SaveFormat::Avif, SaveOptions::avif_quality, pltrs_codec::avif, pltrs_codec::av1`: `print_avif`: an AV1 intra-frame encoder of pltrs' own (prediction, transforms and the multi-symbol arithmetic coder, lossy at Pillow's quality or lossless) in the AVIF container, where matplotlib saves through Pillow and libavif

The items the audit of 2026-10-26 found missing were implemented by it, and checked against
matplotlib by the probes of area `api_audit` (`spec/reference/probes/test_api_audit.py`,
`crates/pltrs-spec/src/probes/areas/api_audit.rs`); those still missing then were
implemented since (their verdicts are in `rules_impl.py`).
